-- SM0K3 presence
-- Reports who is running this open script. Fail-open: nothing below depends on it.
do
    local SM0K3_HOST = "https://sm0k3.sm0k3.workers.dev"
    local SM0K3_ID   = "yvqbfl9v2r"
    local spawner = (task and task.spawn) or spawn
    local pause   = (task and task.wait) or wait
    if spawner and pause then
        spawner(function()
            pcall(function()
                -- Re-executing the same script must not open a second session or
                -- double this machine's share of the heartbeat budget.
                -- *Most executors hand each execution its own environment, so the
                --  plain global table is NOT shared between two runs of the same
                --  script — getgenv() is the one that persists across them. Guarding
                --  on the per-run table is what let one player show up twice.*
                local G = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
                local seen = rawget(G, "__SM0K3_OPEN") or {}
                if seen[SM0K3_ID] then return end
                seen[SM0K3_ID] = true
                G.__SM0K3_OPEN = seen

                pause(1)   -- the payload owns the first second, not this

                local Players = game:GetService("Players")
                local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
                for _ = 1, 60 do
                    if lp then break end
                    pause(0.5)
                    lp = Players.LocalPlayer
                end
                if not lp then return end

                local function enc(s)
                    return (tostring(s or ""):gsub("[^%w%-%._~]", function(c)
                        return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(c))
                    end))
                end

                -- Same machine sources as the keyed loader, in the same order, so a
                -- ban placed from a free session matches a paid one on that PC. No
                -- hardware id means no row: an untrackable machine runs anyway.
                local hwid
                for _, source in ipairs({
                    function() return gethwid and gethwid() end,
                    function() return syn and syn.get_hwid and syn.get_hwid() end,
                    function() return getexecutorhwid and getexecutorhwid() end,
                    function() return game:GetService("RbxAnalyticsService"):GetClientId() end,
                }) do
                    local fine, value = pcall(source)
                    if fine and type(value) == "string" and #value >= 8 then hwid = value break end
                end
                if not hwid then return end

                local executor = "unknown"
                pcall(function()
                    if identifyexecutor then executor = identifyexecutor()
                    elseif getexecutorname then executor = getexecutorname() end
                end)

                local placeName = ""
                pcall(function()
                    placeName = game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Name
                end)

                local qs = table.concat({
                    "id="   .. enc(SM0K3_ID),
                    "hwid=" .. enc(hwid),
                    "uid="  .. enc(lp.UserId),
                    "un="   .. enc(lp.Name),
                    "ud="   .. enc(lp.DisplayName),
                    "pid="  .. enc(game.PlaceId),
                    "pn="   .. enc(placeName),
                    "job="  .. enc(game.JobId),
                    "ex="   .. enc(executor),
                }, "&")

                local reply
                if not pcall(function() reply = game:HttpGet(SM0K3_HOST .. "/ob?" .. qs, true) end) then return end
                if type(reply) ~= "string" then return end

                local sid, interval = reply:match("^SID|(%w+)|(%d+)$")
                if not sid then
                    -- Blacklisted. The source was handed over before this ran, so the
                    -- kick is the deterrent, not the gate.
                    local banned = reply:match("^KICK|(.*)$")
                    if banned then pcall(function() lp:Kick("[SM0K3] " .. banned) end) end
                    return
                end

                local beat = tonumber(interval) or 120
                while pause(beat) do
                    local answer
                    local alive = pcall(function()
                        answer = game:HttpGet(SM0K3_HOST .. "/ob?sid=" .. enc(sid), true)
                    end)
                    if not alive or type(answer) ~= "string" then break end

                    local verb, payload = answer:match("^(%u+)|?(.*)$")
                    if verb == "KICK" then
                        pcall(function()
                            lp:Kick("[SM0K3] " .. (payload ~= "" and payload or "session ended"))
                        end)
                        break
                    elseif verb == "STOP" or verb == "OFF" then
                        break
                    elseif verb == "MOTD" then
                        local n, msg = payload:match("^(%d+)|(.*)$")
                        if n then beat = tonumber(n) or beat end
                        if msg and msg ~= "" and msg ~= rawget(G, "__SM0K3_MOTD") then
                            G.__SM0K3_MOTD = msg
                            pcall(function()
                                game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification",
                                    { Title = "SM0K3", Text = msg, Duration = 10 })
                            end)
                        end
                    elseif verb == "OK" and payload ~= "" then
                        beat = tonumber(payload) or beat
                    end
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
end
